# The journey as a text adventure (perfect child)

Everything the game said and everything the child did, level by level, in play order. Times are game seconds.

## Ninja Training (tutorial)

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja training! First, tap the big gong!
   0.3s   --- tut-gong ---
   0.3s   > child taps: gong
   2.0s   [gong]
   2.0s SENSEI: Stuck? Tap me, down here in the corner. Try it now!
   2.7s   --- tut-help ---
   2.7s   [great]
   2.7s   > child taps: Help
   2.8s SENSEI: That's it! I'm always here to help.
   5.2s   > child taps: Help
   5.4s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   6.4s   --- tut-speaker ---
   6.4s   [good]
   6.4s   > child taps: Hear it again
   6.4s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   8.8s   > child taps: Hear it again
   9.3s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
  11.4s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Bamboo Village w1-wu1: ears

   0.1s   --- warmup ---
   0.1s SENSEI: Ninja ears on! Let's listen to some words.

      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  35.9s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  36.6s   > child taps: rabbit
      🔊 "sun"

      🔊 "cat" (slowly)

      🔊 "cat"

      🔊 "sun" (first sound held) · "sock" (first sound held)
  47.2s SENSEI: Did you notice? Sun and sock start with the same sound...
      🔊 /s/
  53.0s SENSEI: Say that sound with me!
      🔊 /s/
  57.6s SENSEI: This is a sausage.
  59.7s SENSEI: This is the moon.
  61.6s SENSEI: Let me show you!
  62.8s SENSEI: Tap all the pictures that start with...
      🔊 /s/
  67.2s   [good]

  68.5s SENSEI: Your turn!
  69.4s   > child taps: sock
  69.4s   [good]
      🔊 "sock" (first sound held)
  71.8s   > child taps: sausage
  71.8s   [good]
      🔊 "sausage" (first sound held)

  81.0s   [great]
